## 🔧 模块定义

### 1. Sd

#### 基本信息

- **UUID**: `e0a13067-3a19-4a78-9b09-03feb3d11efb`
- **模块名称**: `Sd`
- **类别**: `VENDOR_SPECIFIC_MODULE_DEFINITION`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **支持的变体**:
  - VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - VARIANT-POST-BUILD

#### 模块描述

The Service Discovery module (Sd) implements the SOME/IP-SD protocol to offer and detect available services within a vehicle network in order to establish a service-oriented communication between different network nodes.The Sd module configures the SoAd for the IP data path; Service state changes  are requested from/reported to the BswM module.
#Ethernet #SomeIpSd #SoAd #BswM
#Autosar

#### 配置容器

本模块包含 **23** 个配置容器：

##### 1. SdConfig

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `1bda05ab-6090-4a1c-9e4a-fd69f56bc39e`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SdConfig`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains the configuration parameters and sub containers of the AUTOSAR Service Discovery module.

###### 配置容器

本模块包含 **20** 个配置容器：

####### 1. SdInstance

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `8ce8db39-ab30-4df1-9b19-0e2ed49d5c15`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SdInstance`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

This container represents an instance of the SD; i.e. the SD configuration for a certain link.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **7** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SdMaxNrDestAddr

- **UUID**: `379f47b8-f30e-42f9-91f3-908192d09ea3`
- **参数名**: `SdMaxNrDestAddr`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `16`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This Parameter specifies the size of the address storage array saving the addresses of communication partners.

########## 2. SdInOutQueueSize

- **UUID**: `0537e608-7c07-405a-aef6-f25d14f88572`
- **参数名**: `SdInOutQueueSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `96`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the maximal amount of Entries which can pend in order to be processed. (Specifies the Rx as well as the Tx Entries).

Note: Only (Stop)Subscribe SdEntries are stored in the Rx Queue,
while the TxQueue is used for all SdEntry Types.

########## 3. SdSubscribeEventgroupRetryMax

- **UUID**: `b40607c7-a4d9-46d1-9111-49baa6da8dc4`
- **参数名**: `SdSubscribeEventgroupRetryMax`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Maximum count of retry a subscription, if a subscription to an event group is not acknowledged by SubscribeEventGroupAck or SubscribeEventGroupNack. 0x0=no retry, 0xFF=retry forever (as long as the event group is requested)

########## 4. SdInQueueProcessLimit

- **UUID**: `2061b3e7-212d-4f5b-84d5-00a25c5efeb5`
- **参数名**: `SdInQueueProcessLimit`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies the maximum number of Rx Queue Entries which shall be processed during one MainFunction cycle. If value is set to zero the number of Entries to be processed during one cycle is not restricted.

Note: Only (Stop)Subscribe SdEntries are stored in the Rx Queue.

########## 5. SdInstanceLocalAdressCheckLength

- **UUID**: `584d8531-b961-4cfa-a0f1-5d65acea1f9d`
- **参数名**: `SdInstanceLocalAdressCheckLength`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **取值范围**: [0..128]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies how many leading bits of a remote addresses shall be compared to the local address in order to determine, if the remote address is part of the same subnet and can therefore be used. This shall support IPv4 (0..32) and IPv6 (0..128). A value of "0" means that the address is not checked at all. If this item is not present, the security checks use the configured netmask instead.

######### FLOAT 类型参数

########## 1. SdSubscribeEventgroupRetryDelay

- **UUID**: `7579de90-c8f6-43aa-9e6d-0d0094a1c679`
- **参数名**: `SdSubscribeEventgroupRetryDelay`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0.01`
- **取值范围**: [0.001..50]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Time in seconds when a subscription to an event group shall be retriggered, if no SubscribeEventGroupAck or SubscribeEventGroupNack was received.

######### STRING 类型参数

########## 1. SdInstanceHostname

- **UUID**: `3b8d544e-a507-45a7-9d42-1cda6294e960`
- **参数名**: `SdInstanceHostname`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Configuration parameter to specify the Hostname.

######## 引用关系

此容器包含 **1** 个引用：

######### REFERENCE 类型引用

########## 1. SdPartitionRef

- **UUID**: `d4cd1ef8-7fb7-457e-a8c1-65d6d092011e`
- **引用名**: `SdPartitionRef`
- **引用类型**: REFERENCE

########## 配置容器

本模块包含 **6** 个配置容器：

########### 1. SdEventHandler

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `1ef41162-03dc-40ff-a0d8-5a960ca12835`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SdEventHandler`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Container Element for representing an EventGroup as part of the Service Instance.

############ 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

############# INTEGER 类型参数

############## 1. SdEventHandlerEventGroupId

- **UUID**: `172f5f38-e195-476b-847c-228ccc9d5165`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerEventGroupId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65534]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The EventGroup Id of this EventGroup as a unique identifier of the EventGroup in this service. This identifier is used for EventGroup entries as well.
Please note, that the Eventgroup ID 0x0000 is reserved.

############## 2. SdEventHandlerHandleId

- **UUID**: `a1c30723-69c0-4f93-a581-3af28b551698`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerHandleId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The HandleId by which the BswM can identify this EventGroup.

############## 3. SdEventHandlerMulticastThreshold

- **UUID**: `20010c00-eefb-44d4-80e6-a81c281f46bd`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerMulticastThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies the number of subscribed clients with different endpoint
information (see SWS_SD_00754) that triggers the Server to change
the transmission of events via the Eventhandler Multicast connection.

If configured to 0 only Consumed Evengroup unicast connections and
Consumed Eventgroup multicast connections will be used.

If configured to 1 the first client and all further subscribed clients will be
served via the Eventhandler Multicast connection as configured in
SdMulticastEventSoConRef.

If configured to n up to n-1 clients with different endpoint information
will be served via Consumed Evengroup unicast connections and
Consumed Eventgroup multicast connections. As soon as the number
of subscribed clients with different endpoint information reaches n,
then all subscribed clients are served via the Eventhandler Multicast
connection as configured in SdMulticastEventSoConRef.

This does not influence the handling of initial events.

############## 4. SdEventHandlerMaxRemoteClients

- **UUID**: `f3a1ccd5-992b-437d-8855-486978073154`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerMaxRemoteClients`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The maximum number of remote clients for this SdEventHandler. Can be used to reduce the RAM footprint if the same SoConGroup is used for multiple SdEventHandlers and the maximum number of clients for an individual EventHandler is known during configuration time. If set to zero, the number of maximum clients is calculated according to the number of SocketConnections in the referenced SoConGroups of the EventHandler.

- **描述**: The reference of the SdServerTimer container for this EventGroup.

######### 11. SdEventHandler

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `1ef41162-03dc-40ff-a0d8-5a960ca12835`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SdEventHandler`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Container Element for representing an EventGroup as part of the Service Instance.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. SdEventHandlerEventGroupId

- **UUID**: `172f5f38-e195-476b-847c-228ccc9d5165`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerEventGroupId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65534]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The EventGroup Id of this EventGroup as a unique identifier of the EventGroup in this service. This identifier is used for EventGroup entries as well.
Please note, that the Eventgroup ID 0x0000 is reserved.

############ 2. SdEventHandlerHandleId

- **UUID**: `a1c30723-69c0-4f93-a581-3af28b551698`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerHandleId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The HandleId by which the BswM can identify this EventGroup.

############ 3. SdEventHandlerMulticastThreshold

- **UUID**: `20010c00-eefb-44d4-80e6-a81c281f46bd`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerMulticastThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies the number of subscribed clients with different endpoint
information (see SWS_SD_00754) that triggers the Server to change
the transmission of events via the Eventhandler Multicast connection.

If configured to 0 only Consumed Evengroup unicast connections and
Consumed Eventgroup multicast connections will be used.

If configured to 1 the first client and all further subscribed clients will be
served via the Eventhandler Multicast connection as configured in
SdMulticastEventSoConRef.

If configured to n up to n-1 clients with different endpoint information
will be served via Consumed Evengroup unicast connections and
Consumed Eventgroup multicast connections. As soon as the number
of subscribed clients with different endpoint information reaches n,
then all subscribed clients are served via the Eventhandler Multicast
connection as configured in SdMulticastEventSoConRef.

This does not influence the handling of initial events.

############ 4. SdEventHandlerMaxRemoteClients

- **UUID**: `f3a1ccd5-992b-437d-8855-486978073154`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerMaxRemoteClients`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The maximum number of remote clients for this SdEventHandler. Can be used to reduce the RAM footprint if the same SoConGroup is used for multiple SdEventHandlers and the maximum number of clients for an individual EventHandler is known during configuration time. If set to zero, the number of maximum clients is calculated according to the number of SocketConnections in the referenced SoConGroups of the EventHandler.

####### 4. SdConsumedEventGroup

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `8c409476-7e91-468d-9a3d-c462b2c1e063`

- **容器名称**: `SdConsumedEventGroup`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

A Service may have event groups which can be consumed. A service consumer has to subscribe to the corresponding event-group. After the subscription the event consumer takes the role of a server and the event provider that of a client.

######### 1. SdEventHandler

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `1ef41162-03dc-40ff-a0d8-5a960ca12835`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SdEventHandler`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Container Element for representing an EventGroup as part of the Service Instance.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. SdEventHandlerEventGroupId

- **UUID**: `172f5f38-e195-476b-847c-228ccc9d5165`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerEventGroupId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65534]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The EventGroup Id of this EventGroup as a unique identifier of the EventGroup in this service. This identifier is used for EventGroup entries as well.
Please note, that the Eventgroup ID 0x0000 is reserved.

############ 2. SdEventHandlerHandleId

- **UUID**: `a1c30723-69c0-4f93-a581-3af28b551698`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerHandleId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The HandleId by which the BswM can identify this EventGroup.

############ 3. SdEventHandlerMulticastThreshold

- **UUID**: `20010c00-eefb-44d4-80e6-a81c281f46bd`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerMulticastThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies the number of subscribed clients with different endpoint
information (see SWS_SD_00754) that triggers the Server to change
the transmission of events via the Eventhandler Multicast connection.

If configured to 0 only Consumed Evengroup unicast connections and
Consumed Eventgroup multicast connections will be used.

If configured to 1 the first client and all further subscribed clients will be
served via the Eventhandler Multicast connection as configured in
SdMulticastEventSoConRef.

If configured to n up to n-1 clients with different endpoint information
will be served via Consumed Evengroup unicast connections and
Consumed Eventgroup multicast connections. As soon as the number
of subscribed clients with different endpoint information reaches n,
then all subscribed clients are served via the Eventhandler Multicast
connection as configured in SdMulticastEventSoConRef.

This does not influence the handling of initial events.

############ 4. SdEventHandlerMaxRemoteClients

- **UUID**: `f3a1ccd5-992b-437d-8855-486978073154`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerMaxRemoteClients`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The maximum number of remote clients for this SdEventHandler. Can be used to reduce the RAM footprint if the same SoConGroup is used for multiple SdEventHandlers and the maximum number of clients for an individual EventHandler is known during configuration time. If set to zero, the number of maximum clients is calculated according to the number of SocketConnections in the referenced SoConGroups of the EventHandler.

####### 12. SdEventHandler

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `1ef41162-03dc-40ff-a0d8-5a960ca12835`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SdEventHandler`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Container Element for representing an EventGroup as part of the Service Instance.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SdEventHandlerEventGroupId

- **UUID**: `172f5f38-e195-476b-847c-228ccc9d5165`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerEventGroupId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65534]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The EventGroup Id of this EventGroup as a unique identifier of the EventGroup in this service. This identifier is used for EventGroup entries as well.
Please note, that the Eventgroup ID 0x0000 is reserved.

########## 2. SdEventHandlerHandleId

- **UUID**: `a1c30723-69c0-4f93-a581-3af28b551698`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerHandleId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The HandleId by which the BswM can identify this EventGroup.

########## 3. SdEventHandlerMulticastThreshold

- **UUID**: `20010c00-eefb-44d4-80e6-a81c281f46bd`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerMulticastThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies the number of subscribed clients with different endpoint
information (see SWS_SD_00754) that triggers the Server to change
the transmission of events via the Eventhandler Multicast connection.

If configured to 0 only Consumed Evengroup unicast connections and
Consumed Eventgroup multicast connections will be used.

If configured to 1 the first client and all further subscribed clients will be
served via the Eventhandler Multicast connection as configured in
SdMulticastEventSoConRef.

If configured to n up to n-1 clients with different endpoint information
will be served via Consumed Evengroup unicast connections and
Consumed Eventgroup multicast connections. As soon as the number
of subscribed clients with different endpoint information reaches n,
then all subscribed clients are served via the Eventhandler Multicast
connection as configured in SdMulticastEventSoConRef.

This does not influence the handling of initial events.

########## 4. SdEventHandlerMaxRemoteClients

- **UUID**: `f3a1ccd5-992b-437d-8855-486978073154`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerMaxRemoteClients`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The maximum number of remote clients for this SdEventHandler. Can be used to reduce the RAM footprint if the same SoConGroup is used for multiple SdEventHandlers and the maximum number of clients for an individual EventHandler is known during configuration time. If set to zero, the number of maximum clients is calculated according to the number of SocketConnections in the referenced SoConGroups of the EventHandler.

**容器描述**:

This container represents an instance of the SD; i.e. the SD configuration for a certain link.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **7** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. SdMaxNrDestAddr

- **UUID**: `379f47b8-f30e-42f9-91f3-908192d09ea3`
- **参数名**: `SdMaxNrDestAddr`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `16`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This Parameter specifies the size of the address storage array saving the addresses of communication partners.

######## 2. SdInOutQueueSize

- **UUID**: `0537e608-7c07-405a-aef6-f25d14f88572`
- **参数名**: `SdInOutQueueSize`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `96`
- **取值范围**: [1..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies the maximal amount of Entries which can pend in order to be processed. (Specifies the Rx as well as the Tx Entries).

Note: Only (Stop)Subscribe SdEntries are stored in the Rx Queue,
while the TxQueue is used for all SdEntry Types.

######## 3. SdSubscribeEventgroupRetryMax

- **UUID**: `b40607c7-a4d9-46d1-9111-49baa6da8dc4`
- **参数名**: `SdSubscribeEventgroupRetryMax`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Maximum count of retry a subscription, if a subscription to an event group is not acknowledged by SubscribeEventGroupAck or SubscribeEventGroupNack. 0x0=no retry, 0xFF=retry forever (as long as the event group is requested)

######## 4. SdInQueueProcessLimit

- **UUID**: `2061b3e7-212d-4f5b-84d5-00a25c5efeb5`
- **参数名**: `SdInQueueProcessLimit`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies the maximum number of Rx Queue Entries which shall be processed during one MainFunction cycle. If value is set to zero the number of Entries to be processed during one cycle is not restricted.

Note: Only (Stop)Subscribe SdEntries are stored in the Rx Queue.

######## 5. SdInstanceLocalAdressCheckLength

- **UUID**: `584d8531-b961-4cfa-a0f1-5d65acea1f9d`
- **参数名**: `SdInstanceLocalAdressCheckLength`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **取值范围**: [0..128]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies how many leading bits of a remote addresses shall be compared to the local address in order to determine, if the remote address is part of the same subnet and can therefore be used. This shall support IPv4 (0..32) and IPv6 (0..128). A value of "0" means that the address is not checked at all. If this item is not present, the security checks use the configured netmask instead.

####### FLOAT 类型参数

######## 1. SdSubscribeEventgroupRetryDelay

- **UUID**: `7579de90-c8f6-43aa-9e6d-0d0094a1c679`

- **取值范围**: [0.001..50]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Time in seconds when a subscription to an event group shall be retriggered, if no SubscribeEventGroupAck or SubscribeEventGroupNack was received.

####### STRING 类型参数

- **描述**: Configuration parameter to specify the Hostname.

A Service may have event groups which can be consumed. A service consumer has to subscribe to the corresponding event-group. After the subscription the event consumer takes the role of a server and the event provider that of a client.

######### 1. SdEventHandler

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `1ef41162-03dc-40ff-a0d8-5a960ca12835`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SdEventHandler`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Container Element for representing an EventGroup as part of the Service Instance.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. SdEventHandlerEventGroupId

- **UUID**: `172f5f38-e195-476b-847c-228ccc9d5165`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerEventGroupId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65534]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The EventGroup Id of this EventGroup as a unique identifier of the EventGroup in this service. This identifier is used for EventGroup entries as well.
Please note, that the Eventgroup ID 0x0000 is reserved.

############ 2. SdEventHandlerHandleId

- **UUID**: `a1c30723-69c0-4f93-a581-3af28b551698`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerHandleId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The HandleId by which the BswM can identify this EventGroup.

############ 3. SdEventHandlerMulticastThreshold

- **UUID**: `20010c00-eefb-44d4-80e6-a81c281f46bd`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerMulticastThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies the number of subscribed clients with different endpoint
information (see SWS_SD_00754) that triggers the Server to change
the transmission of events via the Eventhandler Multicast connection.

If configured to 0 only Consumed Evengroup unicast connections and
Consumed Eventgroup multicast connections will be used.

If configured to 1 the first client and all further subscribed clients will be
served via the Eventhandler Multicast connection as configured in
SdMulticastEventSoConRef.

If configured to n up to n-1 clients with different endpoint information
will be served via Consumed Evengroup unicast connections and
Consumed Eventgroup multicast connections. As soon as the number
of subscribed clients with different endpoint information reaches n,
then all subscribed clients are served via the Eventhandler Multicast
connection as configured in SdMulticastEventSoConRef.

This does not influence the handling of initial events.

############ 4. SdEventHandlerMaxRemoteClients

- **UUID**: `f3a1ccd5-992b-437d-8855-486978073154`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerMaxRemoteClients`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The maximum number of remote clients for this SdEventHandler. Can be used to reduce the RAM footprint if the same SoConGroup is used for multiple SdEventHandlers and the maximum number of clients for an individual EventHandler is known during configuration time. If set to zero, the number of maximum clients is calculated according to the number of SocketConnections in the referenced SoConGroups of the EventHandler.

- **UUID**: `1ef41162-03dc-40ff-a0d8-5a960ca12835`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SdEventHandler`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Container Element for representing an EventGroup as part of the Service Instance.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SdEventHandlerEventGroupId

- **UUID**: `172f5f38-e195-476b-847c-228ccc9d5165`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerEventGroupId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65534]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The EventGroup Id of this EventGroup as a unique identifier of the EventGroup in this service. This identifier is used for EventGroup entries as well.
Please note, that the Eventgroup ID 0x0000 is reserved.

########## 2. SdEventHandlerHandleId

- **UUID**: `a1c30723-69c0-4f93-a581-3af28b551698`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerHandleId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The HandleId by which the BswM can identify this EventGroup.

########## 3. SdEventHandlerMulticastThreshold

- **UUID**: `20010c00-eefb-44d4-80e6-a81c281f46bd`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerMulticastThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies the number of subscribed clients with different endpoint
information (see SWS_SD_00754) that triggers the Server to change
the transmission of events via the Eventhandler Multicast connection.

If configured to 0 only Consumed Evengroup unicast connections and
Consumed Eventgroup multicast connections will be used.

If configured to 1 the first client and all further subscribed clients will be
served via the Eventhandler Multicast connection as configured in
SdMulticastEventSoConRef.

If configured to n up to n-1 clients with different endpoint information
will be served via Consumed Evengroup unicast connections and
Consumed Eventgroup multicast connections. As soon as the number
of subscribed clients with different endpoint information reaches n,
then all subscribed clients are served via the Eventhandler Multicast
connection as configured in SdMulticastEventSoConRef.

This does not influence the handling of initial events.

########## 4. SdEventHandlerMaxRemoteClients

- **UUID**: `f3a1ccd5-992b-437d-8855-486978073154`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerMaxRemoteClients`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The maximum number of remote clients for this SdEventHandler. Can be used to reduce the RAM footprint if the same SoConGroup is used for multiple SdEventHandlers and the maximum number of clients for an individual EventHandler is known during configuration time. If set to zero, the number of maximum clients is calculated according to the number of SocketConnections in the referenced SoConGroups of the EventHandler.

Ref.

###### 配置容器

本模块包含 **6** 个配置容器：

####### 1. SdEventHandler

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `1ef41162-03dc-40ff-a0d8-5a960ca12835`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SdEventHandler`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Container Element for representing an EventGroup as part of the Service Instance.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SdEventHandlerEventGroupId

- **UUID**: `172f5f38-e195-476b-847c-228ccc9d5165`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerEventGroupId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65534]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The EventGroup Id of this EventGroup as a unique identifier of the EventGroup in this service. This identifier is used for EventGroup entries as well.
Please note, that the Eventgroup ID 0x0000 is reserved.

########## 2. SdEventHandlerHandleId

- **UUID**: `a1c30723-69c0-4f93-a581-3af28b551698`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerHandleId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The HandleId by which the BswM can identify this EventGroup.

########## 3. SdEventHandlerMulticastThreshold

- **UUID**: `20010c00-eefb-44d4-80e6-a81c281f46bd`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerMulticastThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies the number of subscribed clients with different endpoint
information (see SWS_SD_00754) that triggers the Server to change
the transmission of events via the Eventhandler Multicast connection.

If configured to 0 only Consumed Evengroup unicast connections and
Consumed Eventgroup multicast connections will be used.

If configured to 1 the first client and all further subscribed clients will be
served via the Eventhandler Multicast connection as configured in
SdMulticastEventSoConRef.

If configured to n up to n-1 clients with different endpoint information
will be served via Consumed Evengroup unicast connections and
Consumed Eventgroup multicast connections. As soon as the number
of subscribed clients with different endpoint information reaches n,
then all subscribed clients are served via the Eventhandler Multicast
connection as configured in SdMulticastEventSoConRef.

This does not influence the handling of initial events.

########## 4. SdEventHandlerMaxRemoteClients

- **UUID**: `f3a1ccd5-992b-437d-8855-486978073154`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerMaxRemoteClients`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The maximum number of remote clients for this SdEventHandler. Can be used to reduce the RAM footprint if the same SoConGroup is used for multiple SdEventHandlers and the maximum number of clients for an individual EventHandler is known during configuration time. If set to zero, the number of maximum clients is calculated according to the number of SocketConnections in the referenced SoConGroups of the EventHandler.

##### 13. SdEventHandler

**容器属性**:

- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `SdEventHandler`
- **多重性**: [0..infinite]
- **需要索引**: false

**容器描述**:

Container Element for representing an EventGroup as part of the Service Instance.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **4** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. SdEventHandlerEventGroupId

- **UUID**: `172f5f38-e195-476b-847c-228ccc9d5165`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerEventGroupId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65534]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The EventGroup Id of this EventGroup as a unique identifier of the EventGroup in this service. This identifier is used for EventGroup entries as well.
Please note, that the Eventgroup ID 0x0000 is reserved.

######## 2. SdEventHandlerHandleId

- **UUID**: `a1c30723-69c0-4f93-a581-3af28b551698`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerHandleId`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: ECU
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: true
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The HandleId by which the BswM can identify this EventGroup.

######## 3. SdEventHandlerMulticastThreshold

- **UUID**: `20010c00-eefb-44d4-80e6-a81c281f46bd`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerMulticastThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Specifies the number of subscribed clients with different endpoint
information (see SWS_SD_00754) that triggers the Server to change
the transmission of events via the Eventhandler Multicast connection.

If configured to 0 only Consumed Evengroup unicast connections and
Consumed Eventgroup multicast connections will be used.

If configured to 1 the first client and all further subscribed clients will be
served via the Eventhandler Multicast connection as configured in
SdMulticastEventSoConRef.

If configured to n up to n-1 clients with different endpoint information
will be served via Consumed Evengroup unicast connections and
Consumed Eventgroup multicast connections. As soon as the number
of subscribed clients with different endpoint information reaches n,
then all subscribed clients are served via the Eventhandler Multicast
connection as configured in SdMulticastEventSoConRef.

This does not influence the handling of initial events.

######## 4. SdEventHandlerMaxRemoteClients

- **UUID**: `f3a1ccd5-992b-437d-8855-486978073154`
- **参数名**: `SdEventHandlerMaxRemoteClients`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: The maximum number of remote clients for this SdEventHandler. Can be used to reduce the RAM footprint if the same SoConGroup is used for multiple SdEventHandlers and the maximum number of clients for an individual EventHandler is known during configuration time. If set to zero, the number of maximum clients is calculated according to the number of SocketConnections in the referenced SoConGroups of the EventHandler.

######## 3. SdSafeBswChecks

- **UUID**: `77f7556e-ef80-4025-b7ef-70e35221cd21`
- **参数名**: `SdSafeBswChecks`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Selects if safety checks are used. These checks improve robustness as e.g. invalid parameters are checked before function is executed. This attribute has to be enabled for safety projects where this component is mapped to an ASIL partition.

######## 4. SdSetRemAddrOfClientRxMulticastSoCon

- **UUID**: `e717f075-95f9-4224-ad54-04617e7db437`
- **参数名**: `SdSetRemAddrOfClientRxMulticastSoCon`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter specifies whether the remote address of consumed eventgroup multicast socket connection(s) shall be configured to the unicast remote address of the remote server.
If the remote address is set (value == TRUE), the socket connection can only receive events from this remote server. If the address is not set (value == FALSE) and remains at WILDCARD, this socket connection is able to receive events from multiple remote servers.

######## 5. SdSubscribeEventgroupRetryEnable

- **UUID**: `eb6eb5d4-aca0-4b93-a622-da38ce54dab0`
- **参数名**: `SdSubscribeEventgroupRetryEnable`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Switch to enable or disable the retry functionality to subscribe to Eventgroups of ServerServices with TTL set to 0xFFFFFF.

######## 6. SdGetAndResetMeasurementDataApi

- **UUID**: `7a0bca38-97ca-4199-b3ab-a447bd5fe5a2`
- **参数名**: `SdGetAndResetMeasurementDataApi`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter enables/disables the function Sd_GetAndResetMeasurementData() to get/reset measurement data with the number of invalid SdMessages and received SubscribeEventgroupNack entries.

######## 7. SdRuntimeMeasurementSupport

- **UUID**: `28008808-f7b6-4ff4-86ed-dc8f5c083cf2`
- **参数名**: `SdRuntimeMeasurementSupport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: If enabled runtime measurement points are added to the BSW module code and are added to the module configuration of MICROSAR Rtm. The available measurement points can be seen and configured in the Rtm module configuration. 
Runtime measurement should be disabled in production code.

Only available if the Rtm module is part of the project.

######## 8. SdRuntimeErrorReport

- **UUID**: `97f43c34-3220-4f13-847b-4fecac131790`
- **参数名**: `SdRuntimeErrorReport`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `true`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Switches the runtime error notification on or off.
- true: notification is enabled.
- false: notification is disabled.

######## 9. SdFastSubscriptionRecoveryAfterReboot

- **UUID**: `7df1f682-c682-4088-86cf-d3380a63b725`
- **参数名**: `SdFastSubscriptionRecoveryAfterReboot`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This setting changes the behavior when the reboot of a remote SD instance is detected
with a SD message which contains SubscribeEventgroup entries:

Disabled: Local SD implementation aborts all connections to rebooted remote SD endpoint,
even if connections have been initiated after the reboot. The client has not enough time to reconnect,
so the local SD will send SubscribeNack(s) (behavior before version 16.00.00).

Enabled: Local SD implementation does not abort connections which have been initiated
after the reboot and  sends SubscribeAck(s) right away.

Rationale: A detected reboot shall lead to expiration of the state of the remote SD instance before the reboot.
If the remote SD instance sends Subscriptions with the first SD Message after its reboot however,
connections have been initiated after the reboot as a reaction to Offers and it is not necessary to abort them.
Furthermore, if feature is disabled and the connection is aborted due to the detected reboot, re-initiating the connection
will not work if ephemeral ports are used, even if a delay would have been introduced to allow some time for the client to re-initiate the connection,
since the TCP endpoint of the subscribe will not match to the port of the new connection anymore.
This feature has been introduced to migitate this.

####### FLOAT 类型参数

######## 1. SdMainFunctionCycleTime

- **UUID**: `57fa15d7-b9dc-4cf6-a239-03f74755bf57`
- **参数名**: `SdMainFunctionCycleTime`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0.005`
- **取值范围**: [0.0001..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter defines the cycle time in seconds of the periodic calling of Sd main function.

####### STRING 类型参数

######## 1. SdRandomNumberFunctionIncludeFile

- **UUID**: `ea6156bd-c9ea-4863-b89a-69ee6302d62b`
- **参数名**: `SdRandomNumberFunctionIncludeFile`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `Appl_Rand.h`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the header file holding the declaration of the random number generation function defined in 'SdRandomNumberFunction'.

######## 2. SdRandomNumberFunction

- **UUID**: `3ecfe436-b1b1-4af7-8f34-096d70e56bbf`
- **参数名**: `SdRandomNumberFunction`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `Appl_Crypto_GetRandNo`
- **描述**: This parameter defines the function called for the random number generation.

######## 3. SdUserConfigFile

- **UUID**: `8775395f-7cfa-4de7-8f36-ca91108194d2`
- **参数名**: `SdUserConfigFile`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: Reference to an external user configuration file that will be included during generation.
The content of the user configuration file will be added at the end of the generated module configuration file and allows altering or extending the generated code.
Caution: User configuration files can cause the software module to malfunction and must only be used with great care!

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. SdReduceBoolDataByNegationThreshold

- **UUID**: `b34d16f5-2b7a-4bd4-b005-df9fe50c4a76`
- **参数名**: `SdReduceBoolDataByNegationThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..9223372036854775807]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress boolean CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data by using the negation operator.

0: The optimization is deactivated.
>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

########## 2. SdReduceBoolDataByNumericalComparisonThreshold

- **UUID**: `f8f42c95-3a86-4b78-9607-54430e39a76b`
- **参数名**: `SdReduceBoolDataByNumericalComparisonThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **取值范围**: [0..9223372036854775807]
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress boolean CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data by using numerical comparison with other ROM data.

0: The optimization is deactivated.
>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

########## 3. SdReduceBoolDataByNumericalRelationThreshold

########## 4. SdReduceNumericalDataByOffsetThreshold

########## 5. SdReduceNumericalDataByArraySubtractionThreshold

>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

######### BOOLEAN 类型参数

########## 1. SdOutOfBoundsWriteSanitizer

- **UUID**: `002cbf60-4458-44cf-a416-86c5300b3e2c`
- **参数名**: `SdOutOfBoundsWriteSanitizer`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the generation of runtime checks which call a DET error notification function to find easily out of bounds write problems.

FALSE:  no checks are generated in the data access.
TRUE: the data access is enriched with DET checks to validate indexes.

########## 2. SdOutOfBoundsReadSanitizer

- **UUID**: `492a78dd-a149-4721-b44f-86d2a1d6b41f`
- **参数名**: `SdOutOfBoundsReadSanitizer`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the generation of runtime checks which call a DET error notification function to find easily out of bounds read problems.

FALSE:  no checks are generated in the data access.
TRUE: the data access is enriched with DET checks to validate indexes.

########## 3. SdReduceConstantData2Define

- **UUID**: `256edea7-c6fc-4c37-a134-45fe8e47f1dc`
- **参数名**: `SdReduceConstantData2Define`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM arrays as constant define.

FALSE: ROM arrays are generated as data even if all values are identical.
TRUE: ROM arrays are generated as constant define if all values are identical.

########## 4. SdDeduplicateIndirectedData

- **UUID**: `75f20e8d-d7fb-4e42-b0ee-d1c41094feba`
- **参数名**: `SdDeduplicateIndirectedData`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress 0:N relational ROM data in all CONFIG-CLASSES without increasing the runtime.

FALSE: 0:N relational ROM data is not compressed.
TRUE: 0:N relational ROM data is compressed without increasing the runtime.

########## 5. SdShortSymbols

- **UUID**: `dadbe9d3-1b5c-415d-b05a-ffc79024abfd`
- **参数名**: `SdShortSymbols`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate shortened symbol names.

FALSE: symbol names are generated in a human readable style based on the MIP, tags and variant names.
TRUE: symbol names are generated based on the MIP and a CRC32.

########## 6. SdInterfacesForDeactivatedData

- **UUID**: `04757f54-ba18-4e59-9035-7435aab69d5a`
- **参数名**: `SdInterfacesForDeactivatedData`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate bsw data interfaces for deactivated data elements. This is an advantage for the BSW developer to reduce the time to market with a development environment using auto completition and to investigate potential interfaces.

FALSE: data interfaces are not generated if the data elementis deactivated.
TRUE: data interfaces are generated as e.g. emty macros.

########## 7. SdReferringKeysInComments

- **UUID**: `5325d585-acc7-4242-956c-8d6aa0b0cdfd`
- **参数名**: `SdReferringKeysInComments`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to generate referring keys in comments. This is an advantage for the developer to investigate indirections, but this feature reduces the overall readability of the generated data.

FALSE: referring keys are not generated in comments.
TRUE: referring keys are generated in comments.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. SdOutOfBoundsWriteProtectionStrategy

- **UUID**: `38931c60-22bd-4f60-a3e6-fbd888c53e4c`
- **参数名**: `SdOutOfBoundsWriteProtectionStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `INDEX_SATURATION` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `INDEX_CHECKING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to configure a strategy to protect the code to write out of bounds.

NONE: no protection strategy is generated in the data access.
INDEX_SATURATION: arrays are blown up and the data access index is saturated by an appropriate mask. The advantage is the speed of the data access, but own data elements at other indexes of the same variable can be overridden.
INDEX_CHECKING: the data access index is validated by a runtime check. The advantage is that values are never written to incorrect indexes of the data access.

########## 2. SdBoolDataInArrayOfStructStrategy

- **UUID**: `013b95c9-5bc1-45c3-aba4-4d6c8b760d8c`
- **参数名**: `SdBoolDataInArrayOfStructStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `BOOLEAN`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `BOOLEAN` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `BITFIELD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `BITMASKING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to tailor the usage of boolean data in structures in all CONFIG-CLASSES ROM data. The difference between BITFIELD and BITMASKING depends on your compiler options and memory mapping.

BOOLEAN: the datatype of boolean data is native boolean.
BITFIELD: the bitfield type is used and the compiler extracts the boolean data from structures.
BITMASKING: generated masks are used to extract the boolean data from structures.

########## 3. SdDataDeduplicationStrategy

- **UUID**: `2738b9ca-4109-41c8-8c8e-139574464046`
- **参数名**: `SdDataDeduplicationStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITHOUT_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITH_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to taylor the the deduplication of CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE  ROM data.

NONE: The generated data is not deduplicated.
DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITHOUT_CAST: The data is deduplicated without using casts.
DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITH_CAST: The data is deduplicated using casts.

########## 4. SdReduceBoolDataByNumericalOperandStrategy

- **UUID**: `a34f5981-28cf-4165-bb35-79433ae88a28`
- **参数名**: `SdReduceBoolDataByNumericalOperandStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ZERO` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ANY_VALUE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to taylor the CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM bool data with number deduplication mechanisms. A comparison with 0 is very efficient, but a numerical comparison with a value not 0 can be used to increase the ROM data compression rate.

NONE: ROM data deduplications are switched off.
DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ZERO: ROM data deduplications can be applied with the value 0.
DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ANY_VALUE: ROM data deduplications can be applied with any numerical value.

- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `BOOLEAN` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `BITFIELD` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `BITMASKING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to tailor the usage of boolean data in structures in all CONFIG-CLASSES ROM data. The difference between BITFIELD and BITMASKING depends on your compiler options and memory mapping.

BOOLEAN: the datatype of boolean data is native boolean.
BITFIELD: the bitfield type is used and the compiler extracts the boolean data from structures.
BITMASKING: generated masks are used to extract the boolean data from structures.

######## 3. SdDataDeduplicationStrategy

- **UUID**: `2738b9ca-4109-41c8-8c8e-139574464046`
- **参数名**: `SdDataDeduplicationStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITHOUT_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITH_CAST` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to taylor the the deduplication of CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE  ROM data.

NONE: The generated data is not deduplicated.
DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITHOUT_CAST: The data is deduplicated without using casts.
DEDUPLICATE_CONST_DATA_WITH_CAST: The data is deduplicated using casts.

######## 4. SdReduceBoolDataByNumericalOperandStrategy

- **UUID**: `a34f5981-28cf-4165-bb35-79433ae88a28`
- **参数名**: `SdReduceBoolDataByNumericalOperandStrategy`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **作用域**: LOCAL
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ZERO` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ANY_VALUE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **描述**: This parameter is used to taylor the CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM bool data with number deduplication mechanisms. A comparison with 0 is very efficient, but a numerical comparison with a value not 0 can be used to increase the ROM data compression rate.

NONE: ROM data deduplications are switched off.
DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ZERO: ROM data deduplications can be applied with the value 0.
DEDUPLICATE_DATA_WITH_ANY_VALUE: ROM data deduplications can be applied with any numerical value.
